\displaystyle\norm{ \E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} - \Pi^{\calD(W)}_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}\Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}) }_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 6t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}},

(9.10)

\displaystyle\norm{ \E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (D^\dagger_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} - \Pi^{\calI(W)}_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}\Big) \cdot(D^\dagger_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}) }_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W)}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\cdot(D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 6t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}},

(9.11)

Note that in the statement of
Lemma

9.2
,
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
is shorthand for
\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
, and thus the operators inside the
\norm{\cdot}_{\mathrm{op}}
act on
\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}
. We prove
Lemma

9.2
in
Section

11
.

Next, we define the following adversary states.

Definition 32

(Twirled-
W
purification)
.

Define the states
\ket*{\Adv_i^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}
as follows:

\displaystyle\ket*{\Adv_0^{W,\frakD}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\ket*{0^n}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{0^m}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\mathsf{init}(\mathfrak{D})}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},

(9.12)

\displaystyle\mathrm{For}\quad i=1,\dots,t:\quad\ket*{\Adv_i^{W,\frakD}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Big((1-b_{i})\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot W\cdot\mathsf{cC})+b_{i}\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot W\cdot\mathsf{cC})^{\dagger}\Big)\cdot A_{i}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{W,\frakD}_{i-1}}.

(9.13)

For contrast, let us recall the definition of
\ket*{\Adv_i^V}
.

Definition 33

(
V
purification)
.

Define the states
\ket*{\Adv_i^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
for
0\leq i\leq t
as follows:

\displaystyle\ket*{\Adv_{0}^{V}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\ket*{0^n}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}},

(9.14)

\displaystyle\mathrm{For}\quad i=1,\dots,t:\quad\ket*{\Adv_i^{V}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Big((1-b_{i})\cdot V+b_{i}\cdot V^{\dagger}\Big)\cdot A_{i}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{V}_{i-1}}.

(9.15)

Note that because
b_{i}\in\{0,1\}
, in the construction of these purified states, one either queries
V
,
\mathsf{cD}\cdot W\cdot\mathsf{cC}
for
b_{i}=0
or
V^{\dagger}
,
(\mathsf{cD}\cdot W\cdot\mathsf{cC})^{\dagger}
for
b_{i}=1
.
Because
W
and
V
are partial isometries from
Claim

11
and
Claim

15
,

W,W^{\dagger},V,V^{\dagger}
are all equal to applying a projector followed by a unitary. Hence,
\ket*{\Adv_t^V}
,
\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}
are both states with norm at most
1
.

Fact 10

(Norm of the purified states)
.

For any
t\geq 0
,
\ket*{\Adv_t^V}
,
\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}
both have norm at most
1
.

Furthermore, from
Definition

26
,
V
and
V^{\dagger}
take states in the subspace associated with the projector
\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{\leq i,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
to the the subspace associated with the projector
\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{\leq i+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
.
Hence, after
t
queries in total to
V
and
V^{\dagger}
, we have
\ket*{\Adv_t^V}
is in the image of
\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{\leq t}
.
Similarly, from
Fact

7
,
W
and
W^{\dagger}
map states in
\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq i,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
to
\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq i+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
. Hence, after
t
queries to
W
and
W^{\dagger}
, we have
\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}
is in the image of
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}
.
We collect these two basic properties in
Fact

11
.

Fact 11

(Spaces that the purified states are in)
.

For any
t\geq 0
, we have the following guarantees:

•

\ket*{\Adv_t^V}
is in the image of
\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{\leq t}
.

•

\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}
is in the image of
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}
.

The main technical claim of this subsection is the following.

Claim 18
.

For any integer
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\right]\geq 1-\frac{35t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}

(9.16)

Proof of
Claim

18
.

We prove this claim by induction. When
t=0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_0^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{0^n}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)

(9.17)

\displaystyle=\ket*{0^n}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}

(9.18)

\displaystyle=\ket*{A_0^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},

(9.19)

where the first equality is by the definition of
\ket*{\Adv_0^V}
(
Definition

33
), the second is because
\mathsf{cQ}
acts as identity on
\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
, and the third equality is the definition of
\ket*{\Adv_0^{W,\frakD}}
(
Definition

32
). This implies that

\displaystyle\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_0^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_0^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\right]=1,

(9.20)

so the base case holds.

For the inductive step, assume that

\displaystyle\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\right]\geq 1-\frac{35t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}

(9.21)

for some integer
t\geq 0
. We will prove that the claim holds for
t+1
. To simplify notation, let us assume that the adversary makes a forward query at step
t+1
, i.e.,
b_{t+1}=0
; this is without loss of generality because the argument is symmetric if the adversary makes an inverse query at step
t+1
. We have

\displaystyle\ket*{\Adv_{t+1}^{W,\frakD}}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{cD}\cdot W\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}},

(9.22)

\displaystyle\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_{t+1}^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}

(9.23)

and thus

\displaystyle\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_{t+1}^{W,\frakD}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_{t+1}^V}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\Big)\right]

(9.24)

\displaystyle=\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\right]

(9.25)

By
Fact

11
, the states
\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}
and
\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}
are both in the image of
\Pi_{\leq t}
. Following
Notation

9
, we write
W_{\leq t}=W\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}
and
V_{\leq t}=V\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}
. We can then rewrite (
9.25
) as

\displaystyle(\ref{eq:overlap-twirled-W-Q-plain-V-expand-1})

\displaystyle=\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\right]

(9.26)

Next, we will write
\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}
as

\displaystyle\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}=\mathsf{cD}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}.+\Big(\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}-\mathsf{cD}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\Big)

(9.27)

This allows us to rewrite (
9.26
) as

\displaystyle\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\mathsf{cQ}\cdot V_{\leq t}-\mathsf{cD}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\Big)\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\right].

(9.28)

We can lower bound the second term in the sum as follows. We know that
A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}
and
\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}
have at most unit norm by
Fact

10
and the fact that
A_{t+1},\mathsf{cC},\mathsf{cD},W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}
all have operator norm at most
1
(since
W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}=(W\cdot\Pi_{\leq t})^{\dagger}
and
W
is a partial isometry by
Claim

11
). Then by
Claim

16
, the second term can be lower bounded by

\displaystyle-\norm{\Big( \mathsf{cD} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot\mathsf{cC} \cdot\mathsf{cQ} - \mathsf{cQ} \cdot V_{\leq t} \Big)}_{\mathrm{op}}

(9.29)

\displaystyle-\norm{\sum_{C,D} \Big( D_{\gsA} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V_{\leq t} \Big) \otimes\ketbra*{C,D}}_{\mathrm{op}}

(9.30)

\displaystyle-\max_{C,D}\norm{ D_{\gsA} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V_{\leq t}}_{\mathrm{op}}

(9.31)

\displaystyle\geq-16\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(by
Claim

16
)

Combining this bound with the sequence of equalities
(\ref{eq:overlap-twirled-W-Q-plain-V-expand-0})=(\ref{eq:overlap-twirled-W-Q-plain-V-expand-1})=(\ref{eq:overlap-twirled-W-Q-plain-V-expand-2})=(\ref{eq:overlap-twirled-W-Q-plain-V-expand-3})
, we get

\displaystyle\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_{t+1}^{W,\frakD}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_{t+1}^V}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\Big)\right]

(9.32)

\displaystyle\geq\underbrace{\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot V_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\right]}_{\coloneqq\gamma_{t}}-16\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(9.33)

Next we can use properties of the
W
and
V
operators to rewrite

\displaystyle W_{\leq t}^{\dagger}\cdot V_{\leq t}

\displaystyle=\Big(W\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}\Big)^{\dagger}\cdot V\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}

(9.34)

\displaystyle=\Pi_{\leq t}\cdot W^{\dagger}\cdot V\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}

(9.35)

\displaystyle=\Pi_{\leq t}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}

(by
Corollary

8.3
)

\displaystyle=\Pi_{\leq t}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}-(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)})\Big)\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}

(9.36)

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}\Big)

(
Definition

23
and
Claim

12
)

Plugging this into
\gamma_{t}
, we get

\displaystyle\gamma_{t}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\right]}_{\coloneqq\alpha_{t}}

(9.37)

\displaystyle\quad-\underbrace{\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}\Big)\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\right]}_{\coloneqq\beta_{t}}

(9.38)

Bounding
\alpha_{t}
.

Observe that

\displaystyle\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}

(9.39)

\displaystyle=\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}

(
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
commutes with
(A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger})_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}
)

\displaystyle=\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot A_{t+1}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}.

(by
Fact

11
)

Thus,

\displaystyle\alpha_{t}=\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\right]\geq 1-\frac{35t^{2}}{N^{1/4}},

(9.40)

by the inductive hypothesis.

Bounding
\beta_{t}
.

We will lower bound
-\beta_{t}
by upper bounding
\beta_{t}
:

\displaystyle\beta_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\absolutevalue{\bra*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}} \cdot A_{t+1}^\dagger\cdot\scC^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t} - \Pi^{\calD(W)}_{\leq t}\Big) \cdot\mathsf{cC} \cdot\mathsf{cQ} \cdot A_{t+1} \cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}} \ket*{\init(\frakD)}}

(9.41)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket*{u}\in\mathcal{H}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}:\norm{\ket*{u}}_{2}\leq 1\\
\ket*{v}\in\mathcal{H}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}:\norm{\ket*{v}}_{2}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\bra*{u} \cdot\Big( \Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t} - \Pi^{\calD(W)}_{\leq t}\Big) \cdot\mathsf{cC} \cdot\mathsf{cQ} \cdot\ket*{v} \ket*{\init(\frakD)}},

(9.42)

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket*{v}\in\mathcal{H}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}:\\
\norm{\ket*{v}}_{2}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\bra*{v}\bra*{\init(\frakD)}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}\Big)\cdot\mathsf{cC}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\ket*{v}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\Bigg)^{1/2}

(9.43)

\displaystyle=\norm{ \E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t} - \Pi^{\calD(W)}_{\leq t}\Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}) }_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}^{1/2}

(9.44)

\displaystyle\leq\Big(6t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}}\Big)^{1/2}\leq\frac{3t^{3/4}}{N^{1/4}}

(9.45)

where:

•

the first inequality uses the fact that
\real(z)\leq\absolutevalue{z}
,

•

the second inequality holds because
\mathsf{cC}\cdot A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}\in\mathcal{H}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}
and
A_{t+1}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{V}}\in\mathcal{H}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
both have at most unit norm,

•

the third line uses the fact that

\displaystyle\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ket*{u}:\norm{\ket*{u}}_{2}\leq 1,\\
\ket*{v}:\norm{\ket*{v}}_{2}\leq 1\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\bra*{u} \cdot M \cdot\ket*{v}}=\Big(\max_{\ket*{v}:\norm{\ket*{v}}_{2}\leq 1}\bra*{v}\cdot M^{\dagger}\cdot M\cdot\ket*{v}\Big)^{1/2},

(9.46)

and the fact that
\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}\Big)^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}\Big)=\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}
, since
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}
is a projector.
7
7

7

By
Fact

7
,
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}
is a projector. By
Claim

12
,
\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}
commutes with
\Pi_{\leq t}
and by
Claim

12
,
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}
commutes with
\Pi_{\leq t}
by
Definition

23
. Recall the fact that if
\Pi_{1}
and
\Pi_{2}
are projectors such that
[\Pi_{1},\Pi_{2}]=0
, then
\Pi_{1}\cdot\Pi_{2}
is a projector. Thus, since
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}=(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)})\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}
, we have that
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t}
is a projector.

•

the fourth line follows from the definitions of
\ket*{\init(\frakD)},\mathsf{cC},\mathsf{cQ}
(
Definitions

30
and
31
),

•

and the last line follows from
Lemma

9.2
.

Note that in the fourth line, we can drop the
\mathsf{B}
register since the operator inside the
\norm{\cdot}_{\mathrm{op}}
acts as identity on
\mathsf{B}
.
Putting everything together, we have

\displaystyle\real\left[\bra*{\Adv_{t+1}^{W,\frakD}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_{t+1}^V}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}\Big)\right]

\displaystyle\geq\alpha_{t}-\beta_{t}-16\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}

(9.47)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{35t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}-\frac{3t^{3/4}}{N^{1/4}}-16\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}

(9.48)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{1}{N^{1/4}}\cdot\Big(35t^{2}+3t^{3/4}+16\cdot\frac{2t}{N^{1/4}}\Big)

(9.49)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{1}{N^{1/4}}\cdot\Big(35t^{2}+35t\Big)

(9.50)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{35(t+1)^{2}}{N^{1/4}},

(9.51)

which establishes the claim for
t+1
. This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.3
.

For any
0\leq t<N
and any unitary
2
-design
\mathfrak{D}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{TD}(\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{V}_t}{\Adv^{V}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})\leq\frac{9t}{N^{1/8}}.

(9.52)

Proof.

Using the fact that
\ket*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}
and
\ket*{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
are subnormalized states from
Fact

10
and that
\mathsf{TD}(\outerproduct*{u}{u},\outerproduct*{v}{v})\leq\norm{ \ket*{u} - \ket*{v} }_{2}
for subnormalized states
\ket*{u},\ket*{v}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{TD}\left(\outerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^V}{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{\init(\frakD)}{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}^{\dagger}\right)^{2}

(9.53)

\displaystyle\leq\norm{\ket*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{\gsA\gsB\gsL\gsR} - \scQ_{\gsL\gsR\gsC\gsD} \cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_t^V}_{\gsA\gsB\gsL\gsR} \otimes\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{\gsC\gsD}\Big) }_{2}^{2}

(9.54)

\displaystyle=\innerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}+\innerproduct*{\Adv^V_t}{\Adv^V_t}-2\real\left[\bra*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\otimes\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\right]

(9.55)

\displaystyle\leq 2-2\cdot(1-\frac{35t^{2}}{N^{1/4}})=\frac{70t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}.

(Using
Claim

18
)

Therefore, using the fact that
\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}
acts only on
\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R},\mathsf{C},\mathsf{D}
and
\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}
is a unitary, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathsf{TD}(\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{V}_t}{\Adv^{V}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})

(9.56)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{TD}\Big(\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\left[\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^V}{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{\init(\frakD)}{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}^{\dagger}\right]\Big)

(9.57)

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{TD}\left(\outerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^V}{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{\init(\frakD)}{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}^{\dagger}\right)

(9.58)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{70t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}}\leq\frac{9t}{N^{1/8}}.

(9.59)

This completes the proof.
∎

9.3
Twirled
W
and twirled
\mathsf{pfO}
are indistinguishable

Let
\ket*{+_{N!}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}}
and
\ket*{+_{3^N}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}}
denote the uniform superposition over all permutations and functions, respectively.
We define the follow state obtained by querying twirled
\mathsf{pfO}
.

Definition 34

(Twirled
\mathsf{pfO}
purification)
.

Let

\displaystyle\ket*{\Adv_{0}^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\coloneqq\ket*{0^n}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{0^m}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}}\ket*{+_{N!}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}}\ket*{+_{3^N}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}}\ket*{\mathsf{init}(\mathfrak{D})}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},

(9.60)

For
1\leq i\leq t
, define

\displaystyle\ket*{\Adv_i^{\spfo,\frakD}}\coloneqq\Big((1-b_{i})\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}\cdot\mathsf{cC})+b_{i}\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}\cdot\mathsf{cC})^{\dagger}\Big)\cdot A_{i}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{\spfo,\frakD}_{i-1}}.

(9.61)

To connect twirled
W
and twirled
\mathsf{pfO}
, we need to define the following projections.

Definition 35
.

Define the projectors

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Pi}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathsf{Comp}^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{Comp},

(9.62)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Pi}^{\mathcal{I}(W)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathsf{Comp}^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{Comp}.

(9.63)

We define the following state obtained by querying twirled
\mathsf{pfO}
, but depending on whether forward or inverse query (determined by
b_{i}
) is made, we will add a projector.

Definition 36

(Twirled projected
\mathsf{pfO}
purification)
.

Let
\ket*{\Adv_0^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}\coloneqq\ket*{\Adv_0^{\spfo,\frakD}}
. For
1\leq i\leq t
, define

\displaystyle\ket*{\Adv_i^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}\coloneqq\Big((1-b_{i})\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}\cdot\widetilde{\Pi}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{cC})+b_{i}\cdot(\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}^{\dagger}\cdot\widetilde{\Pi}^{\mathcal{I}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger})\Big)\cdot A_{i}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}_{i-1}}.

(9.64)

Claim 19
.

For all integers
0\leq t\leq N
,

\displaystyle\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}=\mathsf{Comp}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}.

(9.65)

Proof.

We prove this using induction. The base case
t=0
follows from the fact that

\mathsf{Comp}\cdot\ket*{+_{N!}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}}\ket*{+_{3^N}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}}=\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{\varnothing}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(9.66)

If
\ket*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}=\mathsf{Comp}\cdot\ket*{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}
for
t>0
, then we have

\displaystyle\ket*{\Adv_{t+1}^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}

(9.67)

\displaystyle=\Big((1-b_{i})\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot W\cdot\mathsf{cC})+b_{i}\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot W\cdot\mathsf{cC})^{\dagger}\Big)\cdot A_{i}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{W,\frakD}_{t}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}

(9.68)

\displaystyle=\Big((1-b_{i})\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot\mathsf{Comp}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}\cdot\mathsf{Comp}^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{cC})

\displaystyle\,\,\,+b_{i}\cdot(\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{Comp}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{Comp}^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger})\Big)\cdot A_{i}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{W,\frakD}_{t}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}

(Using
Claim

13
)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{Comp}\cdot\Big((1-b_{i})\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}\cdot\widetilde{\Pi}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{cC})

\displaystyle\quad\,\,\,+b_{i}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}^{\dagger}\cdot\widetilde{\Pi}^{\mathcal{I}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}\Big)\cdot A_{i}\cdot\mathsf{Comp}^{\dagger}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{W,\frakD}_{t}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}

(9.69)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{Comp}\cdot\Big((1-b_{i})\cdot(\mathsf{cD}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}\cdot\widetilde{\Pi}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{cC})

\displaystyle\quad\,\,\,+b_{i}\cdot\mathsf{cC}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{pfO}^{\dagger}\cdot\widetilde{\Pi}^{\mathcal{I}(W)}\cdot\mathsf{cD}^{\dagger}\Big)\cdot A_{i}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}_{t}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}

(inductive hypothesis)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{Comp}\cdot\ket*{\Adv^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}_{t+1}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}.

(9.70)

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.4

(Norm bound)
.

For any
0\leq t<N
and any unitary
2
-design
\mathfrak{D}
, we have

\displaystyle 1\geq\innerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\geq 1-\frac{70t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}.

(9.71)

Proof.

We can utilize the following bounds,

\displaystyle\innerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}

(9.72)

\displaystyle\geq\innerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\innerproduct*{\Adv_t^V}{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(
\innerproduct*{\Adv_t^V}{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\leq 1
from
Fact

10
)

\displaystyle=\innerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\bra*{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\bra*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\cdot\mathsf{cQ}^{\dagger}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)

(9.73)

\displaystyle\geq\left|\bra*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\right|^{2}

(Cauchy-Schwarz inequality)

\displaystyle\geq\real\left[\bra*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\mathsf{cQ}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{\Adv_t^V}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\init(\frakD)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\Big)\right]^{2}

(9.74)

\displaystyle\geq(1-\frac{35t^{2}}{N^{1/4}})^{2}\geq 1-\frac{70t^{2}}{N^{1/4}},

(Using
Claim

18
)

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 9.5
.

For all integers
0\leq t\leq N
,

\mathsf{TD}\left(\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\right)\leq\frac{9t^{2}}{N^{1/8}}.

(9.75)

Proof.

Because
\mathsf{Comp}
acts on registers
\mathsf{P},\mathsf{F}
and maps to
\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}
, we have

\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}=\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}.

(9.76)

Because
\mathsf{pfO}
is an isometry,
\ket*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}
has norm
1
.
Furthermore, from
Claim

19
, because
\mathsf{Comp}
is an isometry, we have

\innerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}=\innerproduct{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\leq 1.

(9.77)

Together, we can obtain the following,

\displaystyle\mathsf{TD}\left(\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\right)

(9.78)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{TD}\left(\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{-\mathsf{A}\mathsf{B}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\right)

(9.79)

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{TD}\left(\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\right)

(9.80)

\displaystyle\leq\norm{\ket*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{\gsA\gsB\gsP\gsF\gsC\gsD} - \ket*{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}_{\gsA\gsB\gsP\gsF\gsC\gsD}}_{2}

(
\frac{1}{2}\norm{u u^\dagger- v v^\dagger}_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq\norm{u - v}_{2}
if
\norm{u}_{2},\norm{v}_{2}\leq 1
)

\displaystyle\leq t\cdot\sqrt{1-\innerproduct{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\widetilde{\spfo},\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}}

(
Lemma

2.3
on sequential gentle measurement)

\displaystyle=t\cdot\sqrt{1-\innerproduct{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}}

(
Claim

19
)

\displaystyle\leq t\cdot\sqrt{\frac{70t^{2}}{N^{1/4}}}\leq\frac{9t^{2}}{N^{1/8}}.

(
Lemma

9.4
)

This concludes the proof.
∎

9.4
Proof of
Lemma

9.1

From
Claim

6
, we have

\Tr_{\mathsf{P}\mathsf{F}\mathsf{C}\mathsf{D}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}=\E_{\mathcal{O}\leftarrow\mathsf{sPRU}(\mathfrak{D})}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\calO}}{\Adv_t^{\calO}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}}.

(9.81)

From
Lemma

9.3
, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{TD}\left(\Tr_{\mathsf{L}\mathsf{R}\mathsf{C}\mathsf{D}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{\mathsf{L}\mathsf{R}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{V}_t}{\Adv^{V}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\right)\leq\frac{9t}{N^{1/8}}.

(9.82)

From
Lemma

9.5
, we have

\mathsf{TD}\left(\Tr_{\mathsf{P}\mathsf{F}\mathsf{C}\mathsf{D}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{\mathsf{L}\mathsf{R}\mathsf{C}\mathsf{D}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\right)\leq\frac{9t^{2}}{N^{1/8}}.

(9.83)

By triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{TD}\left(\E_{\mathcal{O}\leftarrow\mathsf{sPRU}(\mathfrak{D})}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\calO}}{\Adv_t^{\calO}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}},\Tr_{\mathsf{L}\mathsf{R}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{V}}{\Adv_t^{V}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\right)

(9.84)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{TD}\left(\Tr_{\mathsf{P}\mathsf{F}\mathsf{C}\mathsf{D}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{\mathsf{L}\mathsf{R}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{V}}{\Adv_t^{V}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\right)

(9.85)

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{TD}\left(\Tr_{\mathsf{P}\mathsf{F}\mathsf{C}\mathsf{D}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{\spfo,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{P}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{F}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{\mathsf{L}\mathsf{R}\mathsf{C}\mathsf{D}}\outerproduct*{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}{\Adv_t^{W,\frakD}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathsf{TD}\left(\Tr_{\mathsf{L}\mathsf{R}\mathsf{C}\mathsf{D}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}{\Adv^{W, \mathfrak{D}}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{C}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{D}}}},\Tr_{\mathsf{L}\mathsf{R}}\outerproduct*{\Adv^{V}_t}{\Adv^{V}_t}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{B}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\right)

(9.86)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9t(t+1)}{N^{1/8}}.

(9.87)

This completes the proof of
Lemma

9.1
.

10
Proof of
Claim

16

In this section, we prove
Claim

16
, which states that the symmetric path recording oracle
V
is approximately unitary invariant. For convenience, we restate the lemma below:

Lemma 10.1

(
Claim

16
, restated)
.

For any
0\leq t<N
, and any pair of
n
-qubit unitaries
C,D
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}
- Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V_{\leq t}}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 16\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}},

(10.1)

\displaystyle\norm{C_{\gsA}^\dagger\cdot(V^\dagger)_{\leq t} \cdot D_{\gsA}^\dagger\otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}
- Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot(V^\dagger)_{\leq t}}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 16\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}},

(10.2)

To prove this lemma, we will define a pair of operators
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
that satisfy
exact
unitary invariance. We will then prove that
E^{L}
is close in operator norm to
V^{L}
, and that
E^{R}
is close in operator norm to
E^{R}
. By combining these guarantees, we will show that
V^{L}
and
V^{R}
satisfy approximate unitary invariance, which we will use to prove that
V
satisfies approximate unitary invariance.

10.1
Defining
E^{L}
and
E^{R}

Definition 37
.

Define the operator
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
that act on registers
\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}
as follows:

\displaystyle E^{L}\coloneqq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in[N]}\outerproduct*{y}{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\sum_{L\in\mathcal{R}}\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1}\cdot\outerproduct*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{R\in\mathcal{R}}\outerproduct*{R}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.3)

\displaystyle E^{R}\coloneqq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in[N]}\outerproduct*{x}{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\sum_{L\in\mathcal{R}}\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{R\in\mathcal{R}}\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(R,(x,y))+1}\cdot\outerproduct*{R \cup\{(x,y)\}}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.4)

We will show that
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
satisfies the following unitary invariance property. To state the property, recall that we define the operator
Q[C,D]
as follows:

Definition 38

(
Definition

27
, restated)
.

For any pair of
n
-qubit unitaries
C,D
, define

\displaystyle Q[C,D]

\displaystyle\coloneqq(C\otimes D^{T})^{\otimes*}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes(\overline{C}\otimes D^{\dagger})^{\otimes*}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.5)

Claim 20

(Exact unitary invariance of
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
)
.

For any pair of
n
qubit unitaries
C,D
, we have

\displaystyle D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}

\displaystyle=Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger},

(10.6)

\displaystyle C^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot E^{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}

\displaystyle=Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot E^{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger},

(10.7)

To prove
Claim

20
, it will be useful to have the following alternative expressions for
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
.

Claim 21

(Alternative form of
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
)
.

The
E^{L}
operator can also be written as

\displaystyle E^{L}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in[N]}\outerproduct*{y}{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\sum_{\ell\geq 0}\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\cdot\Big(\sqrt{\ell+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.8)

\displaystyle E^{R}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in[N]}\outerproduct*{x}{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{r\geq 0}\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\sqrt{r+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{r}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.9)

Here
\Pi_{\ell}
denotes the projector onto the span of length-
\ell
states
\ket*{x_1,y_1,\dots,x_\ell,y_\ell}
, and
(\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell})_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}
is the linear operator that maps

\displaystyle(\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell})_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\cdot\ket*{x_1,y_1,\dots,x_\ell,y_\ell}=\ket*{x,y,x_1,y_1,\dots,x_\ell,y_\ell}.

(10.10)

Proof.

We will prove the statement for
E^{L}
, and the proof for
E^{R}
will be symmetric. To establish
(\ref{def:EL-operator-num-form})=(\ref{def:EL-operator-sym-form})
, we need to prove that for all
(x,y)\in[N]^{2}
and
\ell\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\sum_{L\in\mathcal{R}_{\ell}}\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1}\cdot\outerproduct*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\cdot\Big(\sqrt{\ell+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}.

(10.11)

Since
\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell+1}=\sum_{R\in\mathcal{R}_{\ell+1}}\outerproduct*{R}{R}
(
Notation

5
), we can write the right-hand side of
Eq.

10.11
as

\displaystyle\sum_{L\in\mathcal{R}_{\ell+1}}\outerproduct*{L}{L}\cdot\Big(\sqrt{\ell+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(10.12)

\displaystyle=\sum_{L\in\mathcal{R}_{\ell}}\outerproduct*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}\cdot\Big(\sqrt{\ell+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.13)

Therefore, we need to prove that for all
\ell\geq 0
and
L\in\mathcal{R}_{\ell}
that

\displaystyle\bra*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}\cdot\Big(\sqrt{\ell+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell}\Big)=\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))}\cdot\bra*{L}.

(10.14)

To see this, note that
\bra*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}
is a superposition over all permutations of the elements of
L\cup\{(x,y)\}
, and thus when we right multiply by
\Big(\sqrt{\ell+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell}\Big)
, the resulting state is proportional to
\bra*{L}
. To compute the proportionality constant, note that a

\displaystyle\frac{\binom{\ell-1}{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))-1}}{\binom{\ell}{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))}}=\frac{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))}{\ell}

(10.15)

fraction of the permutations of the elements of
L\cup\{(x,y)\}
will have
(x,y)
in the left-most slot. Thus,

\displaystyle\bra*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}\cdot\Big(\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{\ell}\Big)=\frac{\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))}}{\sqrt{\ell+1}}\cdot\bra*{L},

(10.16)

which gives
Eq.

10.14
when we multiply by
\sqrt{\ell+1}
.
∎

We can use
Claim

21
to prove exact unitary invariance of
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
(
Claim

20
).

Proof of
Claim

20
.

To prove
Eq.

10.6
, it suffices to prove that

\displaystyle D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}=Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.17)

Recall that

\displaystyle Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}=(C\otimes D^{T})^{\otimes*}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes(\overline{C}\otimes D^{\dagger})^{\otimes*}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.18)

Expanding the left-hand-side using the definition of
Q[C,D]
and the expression for
E
given by
Claim

21
, we have

\displaystyle D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(10.19)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in[N]}D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot\outerproduct*{y}{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\sum_{\ell\geq 0}\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\cdot(\sqrt{\ell+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}})\cdot(C\otimes D^{T})^{\otimes\ell}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}

(10.20)

\displaystyle\quad\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot(\overline{C}\otimes D^{\dagger})^{\otimes*}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(10.21)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in[N]}\outerproduct*{y}{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\sum_{\ell\geq 0}\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\cdot(C\otimes D^{T})^{\otimes\ell+1}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\cdot(\sqrt{\ell+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}})

(10.22)

\displaystyle\quad\otimes(\overline{C}\otimes D^{\dagger})^{\otimes*}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(10.23)

\displaystyle=Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(10.24)

A similar argument works for
E^{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
to establish
Eq.

10.7
.
∎

10.2
Approximate unitary invariance of
V^{L}
and
V^{R}

We now prove approximate unitary invariance of the operators
V^{L}
and
V^{R}
. The key step is the following lemma, which relates these operators to
E^{L}
and
E^{R}
.

Recall that for an operator
M
acting on registers
\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}
, the notation
M_{\leq t}=M\cdot\Pi_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
refers to the restriction of the operator
M
to states where the combined length of the
\mathsf{L}
and
\mathsf{R}
components is at most
t
.

Claim 22
.

For any positive integer
t
,

\displaystyle\norm{V^L_{\leq t} - E^L_{\leq t}}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}\quad\text{and}\quad\norm{V^R_{\leq t} - E^R_{\leq t}}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(10.25)

Proof.

We will only prove this for
V^{L}_{\leq t}
, as the proof for
V^{R}_{\leq t}
is analogous. Let
\ket*{\psi}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
be an arbitrary unit-norm state in the image of
\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
. In particular,

\displaystyle\ket*{\psi}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\ket*{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(10.26)

where
\alpha_{x,L,R}
is zero whenever
\absolutevalue{L \cup R}>t
. It suffices to show that for any such
\ket*{\psi}
,

\displaystyle\norm{V^L \ket*{\psi} - E^L_{\gsA\gsL\gsR} \ket*{\psi}}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(10.27)

Expanding out
V^{L}\ket*{\psi}
, we get

\displaystyle V^{L}\ket*{\psi}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha_{x,L,R}}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N]:\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}\ket*{R}.

(10.28)

Expanding out
E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\psi}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
, we get

\displaystyle E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\psi}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\frac{\alpha_{x,L,R}}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{y\in[N]}\ket*{y}\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1}\cdot\ket*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}\ket*{R}

(10.29)

Then we have

\displaystyle V^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\psi}-E^{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\ket*{\psi}

(10.30)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\sum_{y\in[N]}\ket*{y}\ket*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}\ket*{R}\Bigg(\frac{\delta_{y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)}}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{L \cup R}}}-\frac{\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1}}{\sqrt{N}}\Bigg)

(10.31)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N]:\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}\ket*{R}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\Bigg)}_{\coloneqq\ket*{v}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\sum_{y\in\imaginary(L\cup R)}\ket*{y}\ket*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}\ket*{R}\Bigg(-\frac{\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1}}{\sqrt{N}}\Bigg)}_{\coloneqq\ket*{w}}.

(10.32)

Note that
\ket*{v}
and
\ket*{w}
are orthogonal, since
\ket*{v}
is a superposition of states
\ket*{y}\ket*{L'}\ket*{R}
where
y
is in
\imaginary(L^{\prime}\cup R)
exactly once, while
\ket*{w}
is a superposition of states
\ket*{y}\ket*{L'}\ket*{R}
where
y
is in
\imaginary(L^{\prime}\cup R)
at least twice. Thus,

\displaystyle\norm{V^L_{\gsA\gsL\gsR} \ket*{\psi} - E^L_{\gsA\gsL\gsR} \ket*{\psi}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\innerproduct{v}{v}+\innerproduct{w}{w}

(10.33)

Bounding
\innerproduct{v}{v}
.

By changing the order of summation, we can rewrite
\ket*{v}
as

\displaystyle\ket*{v}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
(L^{\prime},R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{L'}\ket*{R}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\Big)\Bigg),

(10.34)

and thus

\displaystyle\innerproduct{v}{v}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
(L^{\prime},R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\Big)\Bigg)^{2}

(10.35)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
(L^{\prime},R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\alpha_{x,L,R}}^{2}\Bigg)\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\Big)^{2}\Bigg),

(10.36)

where the last inequality is by Cauchy-Schwarz. We can bound the summand by writing

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{N}-\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}{\sqrt{N(N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)})}}\Big)^{2}

(10.37)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}{\sqrt{N(N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)})}}\Big)^{2}

(since
\sqrt{a}-\sqrt{b}\leq\sqrt{a-b}
when
a\geq b\geq 0
)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\frac{\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}{N(N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)})}

(10.38)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\absolutevalue{L}+1)\cdot\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}{N(N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)})}

(10.39)

where the last inequality uses the fact that for any fixed
L^{\prime}
, there are at most
\absolutevalue{L}+1
choices of
(x,L)
that can satisfy
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\innerproduct{v}{v}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\absolutevalue{L}+1)\cdot\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}{N(N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)})}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
(L^{\prime},R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\alpha_{x,L,R}}^{2}\Bigg)

(10.40)

\displaystyle=\frac{(\absolutevalue{L}+1)\cdot\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}{N(N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)})}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\alpha_{x,L,R}}^{2}\cdot\Big(\sum_{y\in[N]}\delta_{y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)}\Big)

(10.41)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\absolutevalue{L}+1)\cdot\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}{N}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\alpha_{x,L,R}}^{2}=\frac{(\absolutevalue{L}+1)\cdot\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}{N}.

(10.42)

Bounding
\innerproduct{w}{w}
.

By changing the order of summation, we can rewrite
\ket*{w}
as

\displaystyle\ket*{w}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
(L^{\prime},R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{L'}\ket*{R}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\Big(-\frac{\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1}}{\sqrt{N}}\Big)\Bigg).

(10.43)

Thus,

\displaystyle\innerproduct{w}{w}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
(L^{\prime},R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\lvert\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\alpha_{x,L,R}\Big(-\frac{\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1}}{\sqrt{N}}\Big)\Bigg\rvert^{2}

(10.44)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
(L^{\prime},R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\alpha_{x,L,R}}^{2}\Bigg)\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\frac{\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1}{N}\Bigg)

(by Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
(L^{\prime},R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\alpha_{x,L,R}}^{2}\Bigg)\cdot\frac{(\absolutevalue{L}+1)}{N},

(10.45)

where we have used the fact that for any
y,L^{\prime}
, we have the upper bound

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,L):\\
L^{\prime}=L\cup\{(x,y)\},\\
y\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))+1\leq\absolutevalue{L}+1,

(10.46)

since each tuple in
L^{\prime}
increases the value of
\mathsf{num}(L,(x,y))
by
1
for at most one
x
. Thus,

\displaystyle\innerproduct{w}{w}

\displaystyle=\frac{\absolutevalue{L}+1}{N}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\alpha_{x,L,R}}^{2}\cdot\Big(\sum_{y\in[N]}\delta_{y\in\imaginary(L\cup R)}\Big)

(10.47)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\absolutevalue{L}+1)\cdot|\imaginary(L\cup R)|}{N}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N],\\
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2}\end{subarray}}\absolutevalue{\alpha_{x,L,R}}^{2}=\frac{(\absolutevalue{L}+1)\cdot|\imaginary(L\cup R)|}{N}.

(10.48)

Putting everything together, we have that for all
\ket*{\psi}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
in the image of
\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\Pi_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
,

\displaystyle\norm{V^L_{\gsA\gsL\gsR} \ket*{\psi} - E^L_{\gsA\gsL\gsR} \ket*{\psi}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{2(\absolutevalue{L}+1)\cdot\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}{N}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}},

(10.49)

since
\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}\leq t
and
\absolutevalue{L}+1\leq t+1
. This completes the claim.
∎

Claim 23
.

For any positive integer
t
, and any pair of
n
-qubit unitaries
C,D
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V^L_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V^L_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}

(10.50)

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V^{R,\dagger}_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V^{R,\dagger}_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(10.51)

Proof.

We first prove
Eq.

10.50
. Using
Claim

20
together and the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V^L_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V^L_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.52)

\displaystyle\leq\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V^L_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} - D_{\gsA} \cdot E^L_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} }_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\quad+\norm{Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot E^L_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger- Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V^L_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.53)

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot\norm{V^L_{\leq t} -E^L_{\leq t}}_{\mathrm{op}}

(by unitary invariance of
\norm{\cdot}_{\mathrm{op}}
)

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(by
Claim

22
)

Eq.

10.51
follows from a symmetric argument.
∎

Note that with our convention that
M_{\leq t}=M\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}
, the operator
M_{\leq t}^{\dagger}=(M\cdot\Pi_{\leq t})^{\dagger}=\Pi_{\leq t}\cdot M^{\dagger}
is not the same as
(M^{\dagger})_{\leq t}=M^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}
. However, since our
V^{L}
and
V^{R}
operators map
\Pi_{\leq t}
to
\Pi_{\leq t+1}
, we have the following identities,

\displaystyle(V^{L,\dagger})_{\leq t}

\displaystyle=V^{L,\dagger}\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}=\Pi_{\leq t-1}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}=V^{L,\dagger}_{\leq t-1}

(10.54)

\displaystyle(V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}

\displaystyle=V^{R,\dagger}\cdot\Pi_{\leq t}=\Pi_{\leq t-1}\cdot V^{R,\dagger}=V^{R,\dagger}_{\leq t-1}.

(10.55)

As a consequence,
Eq.

10.51
also holds for the “mis-parenthesized” version. In particular, for any positive integer
t
and any
C,D
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot(V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot(V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq 2\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(10.56)

To prove the approximate unitary invariance of
V
, we need to utilize the following basic lemma.

Lemma 10.2
.

Given any operators
A,B,A^{\prime},B^{\prime}
with operator norm bounded above by one, we have

\displaystyle\norm{A \cdot B - A' \cdot B'}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\norm{A - A'}_{\mathrm{op}}+\norm{B - B'}_{\mathrm{op}}.

(10.57)

Proof.

We can prove this lemma via triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\norm{A \cdot B - A' \cdot B'}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq\norm{A \cdot B - A' \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}+\norm{A' \cdot B - A' \cdot B'}_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.58)

\displaystyle\leq\norm{A - A'}_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\norm{B}_{\mathrm{op}}+\norm{A'}_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\norm{B - B'}_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.59)

\displaystyle\leq\norm{A - A'}_{\mathrm{op}}+\norm{B - B'}_{\mathrm{op}}.

(10.60)

This completes the proof.
∎

We start by proving the approximate unitary invariance for the projectors
V^{L}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}
and
V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger}
.

Claim 24
.

For any positive integer
t
, and any pair of
n
-qubit unitaries
C,D
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot\Big(V^L \cdot V^{L,\dagger}\Big)_{\leq t} \cdot D_{\gsA}^\dagger- Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot\Big(V^L \cdot V^{L,\dagger}\Big)_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}

(10.61)

\displaystyle\norm{C_{\gsA}^\dagger\cdot\Big(V^R \cdot V^{R,\dagger}\Big)_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot\Big(V^R \cdot V^{R,\dagger}\Big)_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(10.62)

Proof.

By the definition of
V^{L}
, we have
(V^{L}\cdot V^{L,\dagger})_{\leq t}=V^{L}_{\leq t-1}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}_{\leq t-1}
. We have

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V^L_{\leq t-1} \cdot V^{L,\dagger}_{\leq t-1} \cdot D_{\gsA}^\dagger- Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V^L_{\leq t-1} \cdot V^{L,\dagger}_{\leq t-1} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.63)

\displaystyle=\Bigg\lVert\Big(D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t-1}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\Big)\cdot\Big(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}^{\dagger}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}_{\leq t-1}\cdot D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}^{\dagger}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-\Big(Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t-1}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\cdot\Big(Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}_{\leq t-1}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Big)\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.64)

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg\lVert\Big(D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t-1}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\Big)-\Big(Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t-1}\cdot Q[C,D]^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}
