\displaystyle\quad+\Bigg\lVert\Big(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}^{\dagger}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}_{\leq t-1}\cdot D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}^{\dagger}\Big)-\Big(Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}_{\leq t-1}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Big)\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(by
Lemma

10.2
)

\displaystyle\leq 4\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}

(by
Claim

23
)

The statement for
V^{R}
can be proven similarly. This concludes the proof of this claim.
∎

We can now prove approximate invariance of
V
(
Claim

16
). By unitary invariance of
\norm{\cdot}_{\mathrm{op}}
we can restate lemma
Claim

16
as follows.

Lemma 10.3

(
Claim

16
, restated)
.

For any positive integer
t
, and any pair of
n
-qubit unitaries
C,D
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA}
- Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 16\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}},

(10.65)

\displaystyle\norm{C_{\gsA}^\dagger\cdot(V^\dagger)_{\leq t} \cdot D_{\gsA}^\dagger- Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot(V^\dagger)_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 16\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}},

(10.66)

Proof.

We will prove the first inequality, as the second follows from a symmetric argument. From the definition of
V
, we have

\displaystyle V=V^{L}\cdot(\mathsf{Id}-V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})+(\mathsf{Id}-V^{L}\cdot V^{L,\dagger})\cdot V^{R,\dagger}.

(10.67)

From the definitions of
\Pi_{\leq t}
,
V^{L}
, and
V^{R}
, we note that

\displaystyle(V^{L}\cdot V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}

\displaystyle=V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t},

(10.68)

\displaystyle(V^{L}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}

\displaystyle=(V^{L}\cdot V^{L,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}.

(10.69)

Using this fact and the definition of
V
, we can apply the triangle inequality to obtain,

\displaystyle\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.70)

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg\lVert D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.71)

\displaystyle+\Bigg\lVert D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.72)

\displaystyle+\Bigg\lVert D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot(V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot(V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.73)

\displaystyle+\Bigg\lVert D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot(V^{L}\cdot V^{L,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot(V^{L}\cdot V^{L,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}.

(10.74)

We now bound each of the four terms. The first term
Eq.

10.71
is bounded above by
2\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}
from
Eq.

10.50
.
The third term
Eq.

10.73
is also bounded above by
2\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}
from
Eq.

10.56
.
The second and fourth terms
Eq.

10.72
,
Eq.

10.74
require the use of
Lemma

10.2
.
Hence, we can bound the second term
Eq.

10.72
as follows,

\displaystyle\Bigg\lVert D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.75)

\displaystyle=\Bigg\lVert D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}^{\dagger}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.76)

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg\lVert D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(10.77)

\displaystyle+\Bigg\lVert C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}^{\dagger}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}},

(10.78)

\displaystyle\leq 6\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(10.79)

where we used the fact that
Eq.

10.77
is bounded above by
2\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}
from
Eq.

10.50
and
Eq.

10.78
is bounded above by
4\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}
from
Eq.

10.62
.
Similarly, we can bound the fourth term given
Eq.

10.74
using the same argument to obtain

\Bigg\lVert D_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}-Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot V^{L}_{\leq t}\cdot(V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger})_{\leq t}\cdot Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\dagger}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}\leq 6\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(10.80)

Combining the bounds on the four terms, we obtain

\norm{D_{\gsA} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot C_{\gsA} - Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot V_{\leq t} \cdot Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}^\dagger}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq 16\cdot\sqrt{\frac{2t(t+1)}{N}}.

(10.81)

This completes the proof of the approximate unitary invariance of
V
.
∎

11
Proof of
Lemma

9.2

In this section, we prove
Lemma

9.2
. For convenience, we restate the lemma below.

Lemma 11.1

(
Lemma

9.2
, restated)
.

For any unitary
2
-design
\mathfrak{D}
and integer
0\leq t\leq N-1
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{ \E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} - \Pi^{\calD(W)}_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}\Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}) }_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 6t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}},

(11.1)

\displaystyle\norm{ \E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (D^\dagger_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} - \Pi^{\calI(W)}_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}\Big) \cdot(D^\dagger_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}) }_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W)}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\cdot(D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 6t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}},

(11.2)

In the above expressions,
\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
is shorthand for
\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
, and thus the operators inside the
\norm{\cdot}_{\mathrm{op}}
act on
\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}
.

11.1
The domain and image of
W

In order to prove
Lemma

11.1
, we will first need to give an explicit characterization of the projectors
\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}
and
\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W)}
.

Definition 39
.

Let

\displaystyle\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2},\\
x\not\in\Dom(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x}{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{R}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.3)

\displaystyle\Pi^{\not\in\imaginary}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2},\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{y}{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{R}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.4)

Definition 40
.

Let

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}\coloneqq\outerproduct*{\EPR_N}{\EPR_N}=\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x\in[N]}\ket*{x}\ket*{x}\Big)\cdot\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{y\in[N]}\bra*{y}\bra*{y}\Big).

(11.5)

Notation 13
.

We use the notation
\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}
for the projector on registers
\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}
that applies
\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}
to the registers
\mathsf{A}
,
\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}
(where
i\in[r]
), and acts as identity on the rest of
\mathsf{R}^{(r)}
. The same notation applies for
\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}
.

Fact 12
.

The projectors
\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
and
\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
commute, and moreover

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.6)

Claim 25
.

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0:\\
\ell+r<N\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-\ell-r}\Pi_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.7)

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W)}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi^{\not\in\imaginary}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0:\\
\ell+r<N\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-\ell-r}\Pi_{r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[\ell+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell+1)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.8)

Proof.

By
Fact

8
,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W^{L})}+\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W^{R})}.

(11.9)

To prove
Eq.

11.7
, it suffices to prove

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W^{L})}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.10)

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W^{R})}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0:\\
\ell+r<N\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-\ell-r}\Pi_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.11)

Proof of
Eq.

11.10
.

From the definition of
W^{L}
, its domain is the image of the projector

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W^{L})}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2,\dist},\\
x\not\in\Dom(L,R)\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x}{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{R}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.12)

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\sum_{(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2,\dist}}\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{R}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2},\\
x\not\in\Dom(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x}{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{R}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2,\dist}}\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{R}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)

(11.13)

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.14)

Proof of
Eq.

11.11
.

We can expand out

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W^{R})}=W^{R}\cdot W^{R,\dagger}

\displaystyle=W^{R}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0,\\
\ell+r<N\end{subarray}}\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot W^{R,\dagger}

(11.15)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0,\\
\ell+r<N\end{subarray}}W_{\ell,r}^{R}\cdot W^{R,\dagger}_{\ell,r}

(11.16)

where the second equality uses the fact that the domain of
W^{R}
is contained in the image of the projector
\sum_{\ell,r\geq 0,\ell+r<N}\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
, i.e.,
W^{R}
is only defined on states where the
\mathsf{L}
and
\mathsf{R}
registers have sizes
\ell,r\geq 0
where
\ell+r<N
. Thus, it suffices to prove that for all
\ell,r\geq 0
such that
\ell+r<N
that

\displaystyle W_{\ell,r}^{R}\cdot W_{\ell,r}^{R,\dagger}=\frac{N}{N-\ell-r}\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}},

(11.17)

where we use our notational convention that for an operator
B
acting on a variable-length registers
\mathsf{L},\mathsf{R}
, the operator
B_{\ell,r}=B\cdot\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
is the restriction of
B
to states where the
\mathsf{L}
register is length
\ell
and the
\mathsf{R}
register is length
r
.

We will do this by relating
W^{R}
to the
E^{R}
operator defined in
Definition

37
.
From the definition of
W^{R}
in
Definition

22
, we immediately have:

\displaystyle W^{R}_{\ell,r}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\ell-r}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2,\dist},\\
\absolutevalue{L}=\ell,\absolutevalue{R}=r,\\
x\not\in\Dom(L\cup R),\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x}{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{R \cup\{(x,y)\}}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.18)

Using
Eq.

10.4
for
E^{R}
, we have

\displaystyle E^{R}_{\ell,r}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2},\\
\absolutevalue{L}=\ell,\absolutevalue{R}=r,\\
x,y\in[N]\end{subarray}}\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(R,(x,y))+1}\cdot\outerproduct*{x}{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{L}{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{R \cup\{(x,y)\}}{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.19)

By inspection, we can see that

\displaystyle W^{R}_{\ell,r}=\frac{\sqrt{N}}{\sqrt{N-\ell-r}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot E^{R}_{\ell,r},

(11.20)

since multiplying by
\Pi_{\mathsf{bij}}
restricts the sum in
Eq.

11.19
to
x,y,L,R
such that
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2,\dist}
,
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)
, and and
x\not\in\Dom(L\cup R)
, and for all such
x,y,L,R
, we have
\sqrt{\mathsf{num}(R,(x,y))+1}=1
.
Now, recall from
Eq.

10.9
in
Claim

21
that
E^{R}_{\ell,r}
can be also be written as

\displaystyle E^{R}_{\ell,r}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in[N]}\outerproduct*{x}{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\sqrt{r+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{r}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.21)

And thus, we have the following expression for
E^{R}_{\ell,r}

\displaystyle E^{R}_{\ell,r}\cdot E^{R,\dagger}_{\ell,r}

(11.22)

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x,y\in[N]}\outerproduct*{x}{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\sqrt{r+1}\cdot\ket*{x,y}\otimes\Pi_{r}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{x^{\prime},y^{\prime}\in[N]}\outerproduct*{y'}{x'}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\Big(\sqrt{r+1}\cdot\bra*{x',y'}\otimes\Pi_{r}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)

(11.23)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}\in[N]}\outerproduct*{x}{x'}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big((r+1)\cdot\outerproduct*{x}{x'}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\otimes\sum_{y}\outerproduct*{y}{y}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{Y},1}}}\otimes\Pi_{r}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.24)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}\in[N]}\outerproduct*{x}{x'}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\outerproduct*{x}{x'}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\otimes\sum_{y}\outerproduct*{y}{y}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}\otimes\Pi_{r}\Big)_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}}_{r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.25)

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\Pi_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Big)\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.26)

And thus, combining this with
Eq.

11.20
, we obtain

\displaystyle W_{\ell,r}^{R}\cdot W_{\ell,r}^{R,\dagger}

(11.27)

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N-\ell-r}\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\Pi_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Big)\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.28)

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N-\ell-r}\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\ell,r+1,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.29)

This concludes the proof.
∎

Definition 41
.

Define

\displaystyle P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0:\\
\ell+r<N\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-\ell-r}\Pi_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.30)

\displaystyle P^{\mathcal{I}(W)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0:\\
\ell+r<N\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-\ell-r}\Pi_{r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[\ell+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell+1)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.31)

Combining
Claims

25
,
41
and
12
, we have the following corollary.

Corollary 11.1
.

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}\cdot P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}

(11.32)

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W)}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}\cdot P^{\mathcal{I}(W)}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}

(11.33)

11.2
An operator upper bound

Claim 26
.

For any non-negative integers
\ell,r
,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle\preceq\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\Bigg(\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)+2r\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)

(11.34)

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-P^{\mathcal{I}(W)}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle\preceq\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\Bigg(\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{Y},i}}}\right)+2\ell\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)

(11.35)

Proof.

We will prove the first inequality, and the second will follow from a symmetric argument. We begin by writing out
P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
as

\displaystyle P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell^{\prime},r^{\prime}\geq 0:\\
\ell^{\prime}+r^{\prime}<N\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-\ell^{\prime}-r^{\prime}}\Pi_{\ell^{\prime},{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r^{\prime}+1]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r^{\prime}+1)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.36)

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}+\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\Pi_{\ell,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}},

(11.37)

where the second equality uses the fact that
\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
commutes with every other projector in the above expression. Note that in the special case where
r=0
, the above expression simplifies to

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.38)

Next, we use our expression for
P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
to expand out

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.39)

Using the definition of
\Pi_{\ell,r}^{\not\in\Dom}
, we have

\displaystyle\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\preceq\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}.

(11.40)

This inequality holds because any state in the image of
\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\not\in\Dom}
must have a collision between the
\mathsf{A}
register and at least one of the registers
\{\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}\}_{i\in[\ell]}\cup\{\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}\}_{i\in[r]}
, and will therefore be in the image of at least one of the projectors on the right-hand-side. Plugging this inequality into
Eq.

11.39
, we have

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.41)

\displaystyle\preceq\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.42)

\displaystyle\preceq\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\underbrace{\Bigg(\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Big)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)}_{\mathrm{Term}_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\underbrace{\Bigg(\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\sum_{i\in[r]}\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Big)\Big)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)}_{\mathrm{Term}_{2}}.

(11.43)

Comparing (
11.43
) to the right-hand side of
Eq.

11.34
, it suffices to prove that:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Term}_{1}

\displaystyle\preceq\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}},

(11.44)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Term}_{2}

\displaystyle\preceq\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)+2r\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.45)

Bounding the first term.

To prove
Eq.

11.44
, it suffices to prove for each
i\in[\ell]
,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle\preceq\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}.

(11.46)

This holds because
\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}
commutes with
\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
(since both are diagonal in the standard basis) and thus:

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\preceq\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}=\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}.

(11.47)

Bounding the second term.

To prove
Eq.

11.45
, it suffices to prove for each
i\in[r]
,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Big)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\preceq\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)+2\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.48)

Note that
\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
and
\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Big)
do not commute, so we cannot simply apply the argument we used to bound the first term. However, these two operators
almost
commute. In particular,

\displaystyle\norm{\Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR} \cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}}\Big) - \Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}}\Big) \cdot\Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR}}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}.

(11.49)

We prove
Eq.

11.49
in
Claim

27
, which follows this proof. To simplify notation for the following steps, let us write
A\coloneqq\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
and
B\coloneqq\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}
. Note that
B
is a projector because
\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}
projects onto a subspace of the image of
\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}
.

Using the definition of operator norm, we have

\displaystyle A\cdot B\cdot A-B\cdot A\cdot B\preceq\norm{A \cdot B \cdot A - B \cdot A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\mathsf{Id}.

(11.50)

Adding
B\cdot A\cdot B
to both sides, we get

\displaystyle A\cdot B\cdot A

\displaystyle\preceq B\cdot A\cdot B+\norm{A \cdot B \cdot A - B \cdot A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\mathsf{Id}

(11.51)

\displaystyle\preceq B+\norm{A \cdot B \cdot A - B \cdot A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\mathsf{Id},

(11.52)

where the second inequality uses the fact that
B\cdot A\cdot B\preceq B\cdot\mathsf{Id}\cdot B=B
for projectors
A
and
B
. Now,

\displaystyle\norm{A \cdot B \cdot A - B \cdot A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq\norm{A \cdot B \cdot A - A \cdot B \cdot A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}+\norm{A \cdot B \cdot A \cdot B - B \cdot A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}

(triangle inequality)

\displaystyle=\norm{A \cdot B \cdot(B \cdot A - A \cdot B)}_{\mathrm{op}}+\norm{(A \cdot B - B \cdot A) \cdot A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}

(since
A^{2}=A
and
B^{2}=B
)

\displaystyle\leq\norm{A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\norm{B \cdot A - A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}+\norm{A \cdot B - B \cdot A}_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\norm{A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.53)

\displaystyle\leq\norm{B \cdot A - A \cdot B}_{\mathrm{op}}+\norm{A \cdot B - B \cdot A}_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.54)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}

(by
Claim

27
)

Plugging this inequality into
Eq.

11.52
, and plugging in
A=\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
and
B=\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}
yields
Eq.

11.48
. This completes the proof.
∎

We now prove
Claim

27
, which we used in the previous proof.

Claim 27
.

For any integers
\ell,r\geq 0
such that
\ell+r\leq N
and any index
i\in[r]
(if such an
i
exists
8
8

8

Note that
[r]\coloneqq\{1,2,\dots,r\}
, so no
i
exists when
r=0
. But in this case, the bound is trivially satisfied.
), we have

\displaystyle\norm{\Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR} \cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}}\Big) - \Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}}\Big) \cdot\Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR}}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}.

(11.55)

Similarly, for any integers
\ell,r\geq 0
such that
\ell+r\leq N
, and any index
i\in[\ell]
(if such an
i
exists), we have

\displaystyle\norm{\Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR} \cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sY,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sY,i}}}\Big) - \Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sY,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sY,i}}}\Big) \cdot\Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR}}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}.

(11.56)

Proof.

We will prove the first inequality, and the second will follow from a symmetric argument. Assume without loss of generality that
i=1
. Since
\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
and
\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}
commute,

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\Big)-\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\Big)\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

(11.57)

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}.

(11.58)

We will write down an explicit expression for the operator
\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}
, which we will then use to bound the operator norm.

We can expand out
\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
as

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist},\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x,y}{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{x',y'}{x',y'}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}}},

(11.59)

where in the above sum,
x,y\in[N]^{\ell}
,
x^{\prime},y^{\prime}\in[N]^{r}
, and
\ket*{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}}}=\ket*{x_1,y_1,\dots,x_\ell,y_\ell}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}}}
.

We can then expand out

\displaystyle(\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}

(11.60)

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist},\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x,y}{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{x',y'}{x',y'}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}}}\Bigg)\cdot\Bigg(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{u,v\in[N]}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\Bigg)

(11.61)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]})\in[N]^{\ell+r-1}_{\dist},\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x,y}{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{x'_{[2:r]},y'}{x'_{[2:r]},y'}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}\setminus\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\otimes\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in[N],\\
u\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}},

(11.62)

where
x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\in[N]^{r-1}
, the notation
u\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}
means
u
must be distinct from each element of
x
and
x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}
, and
\mathsf{R}\setminus\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}
refers to all of the registers
\mathsf{R}=(\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{Y}_{1}},\dots,\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},r},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{Y}_{r}})

except
for
\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}
.

Next, to get an explicit expression for
\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
, we can just take the conjugate transpose of
Eq.

11.62
. By exploiting symmetry, we can write the result in such a way that it looks nearly identical to (
11.62
), except for the part highlighted in red.

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\cdot(\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})

(11.63)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]})\in[N]^{\ell+r-1}_{\dist},\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x,y}{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{x'_{[2:r]},y'}{x'_{[2:r]},y'}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}\setminus\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\otimes\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}u\in[N],}\\
{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}v\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}.

(11.64)

Subtracting (
11.62
) from (
11.64
), we get

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\cdot(\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})-(\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}

(11.65)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]})\in[N]^{\ell+r-1}_{\dist},\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{x,y}{x,y}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{x'_{[2:r]},y'}{x'_{[2:r]},y'}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}\setminus\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}

\displaystyle\quad\otimes\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in[N],\\
v\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in[N],\\
u\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}.

(11.66)

Since this is a block diagonal matrix with blocks indexed by
x,y,x^{\prime}_{[2:r]},y^{\prime}
, the operator norm is

\displaystyle\norm{\Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,1}}} \cdot(\Id_{\gsA} \otimes\Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR}) - (\Id_{\gsA} \otimes\Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR}) \cdot\Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,1}}}}_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.67)

\displaystyle=\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}(x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]})\in[N]^{\ell+r-1}_{\dist},\\
(y,y^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\lVert\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in[N],\\
v\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in[N],\\
u\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}.

(11.68)

Fix any choice of
(x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]})\in[N]^{\ell+r-1}_{\dist},(y,y^{\prime})\in[N]^{\ell+r}_{\dist}
. We can rewrite

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in[N],\\
v\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in[N],\\
u\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}

(11.69)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\},\\
v\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\},\\
v\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}\end{subarray}}\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\outerproduct*{u}{v}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}}

(11.70)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{(N-r-\ell+1)(r+\ell-1)}}{N}\Big(\outerproduct*{\phi}{\psi}-\outerproduct*{\psi}{\phi}\Big),

(11.71)

where
\ket*{\psi}
and
\ket*{\phi}
are the following vectors

\displaystyle\ket*{\psi}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-r-\ell+1}}\sum_{u\not\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}}\ket*{u}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{u}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}},

(11.72)

\displaystyle\ket*{\phi}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{1}{\sqrt{r+\ell-1}}\sum_{u\in\{x,x^{\prime}_{[2,r]}\}}\ket*{u}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{u}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},1}}},

(11.73)

Note that if
r=1
and
\ell=0
, these vectors have norm
0
and we are done. For all other choices of
r\geq 1
,
\ell\geq 0
such that
r+\ell\leq N
, these are orthogonal unit vectors, and thus
\outerproduct*{\psi}{\phi}-\outerproduct*{\phi}{\psi}
has operator norm
1
. It follows that the overall operator norm is at most

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{(N-r-\ell+1)(r+\ell-1)}}{N}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}.

(11.74)

which completes the proof.
∎

11.3
An intermediate lemma on
2
-design twirling

Claim 28

(Twirling)
.

For any unitary
2
-design
\mathfrak{D}
and any non-negative integers
\ell,r
,

\displaystyle\norm{\E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{i \in[\ell]} \Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sX,i}}} + \sum_{i \in[r]} \left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} \right) \Bigg) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\Bigg)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\ell+r}{N+1},

(11.75)

\displaystyle\norm{\E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (D^\dagger_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Bigg( \sum_{i \in[r]} \Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} + \sum_{i \in[\ell]} \left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sX,i}}} \right) \Bigg) \cdot(D^\dagger_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{i\in[r]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\Bigg)\cdot(D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2r+\ell}{N+1}.

(11.76)

Before we prove
Claim

28
, let us recall the following claims that were proven in the preliminaries.

Claim 29

(
Claim

1
, restated)
.

For any
n
-qubit unitary
2
-design
\mathfrak{D}
,

\displaystyle\E_{U\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}\Big[(U\otimes U)^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}\cdot(U\otimes U)\Big]=\frac{2}{N+1}\cdot\Pi_{\mathsf{sym}}^{N,2}.

(11.77)

Claim 30

(
Claim

2
, restated)
.

For any
n
-qubit unitary
2
-design
\mathfrak{D}
,

\displaystyle\E_{U\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}\Big[(U\otimes\overline{U})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}\Big)\cdot(U\otimes\overline{U})\Big]=\frac{1}{N+1}\cdot(\mathsf{Id}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}).

(11.78)

Proof of
Claim

28
.

We prove the first inequality, and the proof for the second one is symmetric. By the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\norm{\E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{i \in[\ell]} \Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sX,i}}} + \sum_{i \in[r]} \left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} \right) \Bigg) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}+\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)\Bigg)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.79)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\norm{\E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sX,i}}} \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\in[r]}\norm{\E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} \Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.80)

Plugging in

\displaystyle Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}=(C\otimes D^{T})^{\otimes*}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\otimes(\overline{C}\otimes D^{\dagger})^{\otimes*}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}},

(11.81)

we can rewrite (
11.80
) as

\displaystyle(\ref{eq:big-twirling-lemma-first-step})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\norm{\E_{C \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes C_{\darkgray{\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sX,i}}})^\dagger\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sX,i}}} \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes C_{\darkgray{\sA,\sL^{(\ell)}_{\sX,i}}})}_{C\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes C_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes C_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\in[r]}\norm{\E_{C \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes\overline{C}_{\darkgray{\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}})^\dagger\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} - \Pi^{\EPR}_{\darkgray{\sA,\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}} \Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes\overline{C}_{\darkgray{\sR^{(r)}_{\sX,i}}})}_{C\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\overline{C}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes\overline{C}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.82)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\ell}{N+1}+\frac{r}{N+1}.

(by
Claims

1
and
2
)

This completes the proof.
∎

11.4
Finishing the proof of
Lemma

9.2

We now prove
Lemma

9.2
, which we state again for convenience.

Lemma 11.2

(
Lemma

9.2
, restated)
.

For any unitary
2
-design
\mathfrak{D}
and integer
0\leq t\leq N/2
, we have

\displaystyle\norm{ \E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} - \Pi^{\calD(W)}_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}\Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}) }_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 6t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}},

(11.83)

\displaystyle\norm{ \E_{C,D \gets\frakD} (D^\dagger_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\bij}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} - \Pi^{\calI(W)}_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}\Big) \cdot(D^\dagger_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}) }_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{I}(W)}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\cdot(D^{\dagger}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 6t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}},

(11.84)

Proof.

Using
12
and
11.1
, we have

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}},

(11.85)

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}},

(11.86)

Since
\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}
commutes with
\Pi_{\leq t}
, this implies

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathsf{bij}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}},

(11.87)

\displaystyle\Pi^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot P^{\mathcal{D}(W)}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(11.88)

Plugging this into the left-hand side of
Eq.

11.83
, we get

\displaystyle=\norm{\E_{C, D \leftarrow\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\calR^2}_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot\Pi^{\xydist}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} \cdot\Pi^{\calR^2}_{\gsL\gsR} - \Pi^{\calR^2}_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot P_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}^{\calD(W)} \cdot\Pi^{\calR^2}_{\gsL\gsR} \Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot P_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.89)

\displaystyle=\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\cdot\Bigg(\norm{ \Pi^{\calR^2}_{\gsL\gsR} \cdot\Bigg( \E_{C, D \leftarrow\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\xydist}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} - P_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}^{\calD(W)} \Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR}) \Bigg) \cdot\Pi^{\calR^2}_{\gsL\gsR}}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-P_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})\Bigg)\cdot\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.90)

\displaystyle\leq\norm{ \E_{C, D \leftarrow\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\xydist}_{\leq t, \gsL\gsR} - P_{\leq t, \gsA\gsL\gsR}^{\calD(W)} \Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-P_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}},

(11.91)

where the second equality follows from the fact that
\Pi^{\mathcal{R}^{2}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
commutes with
Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}
, and the inequality uses the fact that
\norm{\Pi\cdot M \cdot\Pi}_{\mathrm{op}}\leq\norm{M}_{\mathrm{op}}
for any projector
\Pi
.

Next, we use the fact that the operators
\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\leq t}
and
P_{\leq t,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}
are block diagonal with respect to
\{\Pi_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\}_{\ell,r\geq 0}
(recall that
\Pi_{\ell,r}
denotes the projector that restricts the registers
\mathsf{L}
and
\mathsf{R}
to have lengths
\ell
and
r
, respectively), i.e., they map the image of
\Pi_{\ell,r}
to the image of
\Pi_{\ell,r}
. Thus,

\displaystyle(\ref{eq:twirling-main-eq-1-upper-bound})

\displaystyle=\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0:\\
\ell+r\leq t\end{subarray}}\norm{ \E_{C, D \leftarrow\frakD} (C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})^\dagger\cdot\Big( \Pi^{\xydist}_{\ell,r, \gsL\gsR} - P_{\ell,r, \gsA\gsL\gsR}^{\calD(W)} \Big) \cdot(C_{\gsA} \otimes Q[C,D]_{\gsL\gsR})}_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Big(\Pi^{\mathrm{dist}_{X,Y}}_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}-P_{\ell,r,{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}^{\mathcal{D}(W)}\Big)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})_{\mathrm{op}}

(11.92)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0:\\
\ell+r\leq t\end{subarray}}\Bigg\lVert\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\cdot\E_{C,D\leftarrow\mathfrak{D}}(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})^{\dagger}\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{i\in[\ell]}\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{L}^{(\ell)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\in[r]}\left(\Pi^{\mathsf{eq}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}-\Pi^{\mathsf{EPR}}_{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A},\mathsf{R}^{(r)}_{\mathsf{X},i}}}\right)+2r\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}\mathsf{Id}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\Bigg)\cdot(C_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\otimes Q[C,D]_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}})\Bigg\rVert_{\mathrm{op}}

(by
Claim

26
)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\ell,r\geq 0:\\
\ell+r\leq t\end{subarray}}\frac{N}{N-\ell-r+1}\cdot\Bigg(\frac{2\ell+r}{N+1}+2r\sqrt{\frac{\ell+r}{N}}\Bigg).

(by
Claim

28
and the triangle inequality)

This expression is maximized by setting
r=t
and
\ell=0
, which yields a final upper bound of

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N-t+1}\cdot\Bigg(\frac{t}{N+1}+2t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}}\Bigg)\leq\frac{N}{N-t}\cdot\Bigg(3t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}}\Bigg)\leq 6t\sqrt{\frac{t}{N}}

(11.93)

where the last inequality uses the assumption that
t\leq N/2
.
∎

Part III
Appendices

Appendix A
Efficient circuit implementation of path-recording oracle

We briefly describe how to efficiently implement the path-recording oracles on a quantum computer to simulate forward and inverse queries of a Haar-random unitary up to inverse-exponential error.

A.1
Implementing relation states

There are multiple ways to implement the relation state
\ket*{R}
for a relation
R
.
We describe one simple approach.
We represent
\ket*{R}
using
|R|

2n
-qubit registers by sorting the tuples
(x,y)\in[N]^{2}
in the relation
R
.
For example, consider
R
, we can store
\ket*{R}
on a quantum computer as

\ket*{x_1}\ket*{y_1}\ldots\ket*{x_{|R|}}\ket*{y_{|R|}},

(A.1)

where
R=\{(x_{i},y_{i})\}_{i=1}^{|R|}
and
(x_{1},y_{1})\leq\ldots\leq(x_{|R|},y_{|R|})
. Here,
(x,y)\leq(x^{\prime},y^{\prime})
denotes the lexicographical ordering, which means either (a)
x<x^{\prime}
or (b)
x=x^{\prime}
and
y\leq y^{\prime}
.

A.2
Implementing forward queries

In
Section

4
, we defined a (standard) path-recording oracle
V
that simulates forward (but not inverse) queries to a Haar-random unitary. In this subsection, we describe how to implement this linear map efficiently.

Definition 42
.

[
Definition

10
, repeated]
The path-recording oracle
V
is a linear map
V:\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{A}}\otimes\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{R}}\rightarrow\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{A}}\otimes\mathcal{H}_{\mathsf{R}}
defined as follows. For all
x\in[N]
and injective relations
R\in\mathcal{R}^{\mathsf{inj}}
such that
\absolutevalue{R}<N
,

V:\ket*{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\mapsto\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{R}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
y\not\in\imaginary(R)\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{R \cup\{(x,y)\}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(A.2)

We briefly sketch how to implement
V
on an input
\ket*{x}\ket*{R}
.

Input:
a state of the form
\ket*{x}\ket*{R}
.

1.

The first step is to perform the map

\displaystyle\ket*{x}\ket*{R}\mapsto\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{R}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
y\not\in\imaginary(R)\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{x}\ket*{R}.

(A.3)

This can be done as follows:

(a)

First, prepare a new register containing a uniform superposition over
\{1,2,\dots,N-\absolutevalue{R}\}
.

(b)

Next, observe that given
y_{1}\leq\dots\leq y_{\absolutevalue{R}}
(which are stored in subregisters of
\ket*{R}
), there is an efficiently computable bijection
f
from
\{1,2,\dots,N-\absolutevalue{R}\}
to the set
\{y:y\in[N],y\not\in\imaginary(R)\}
: on input
x
, compute the number
n_{x}
of elements
y_{i}
in the list
(y_{1},\dots,y_{\absolutevalue{R}})
such that
x\geq y_{i}
, and output
x+n_{x}
. Using a similar algorithm, we can also compute
f^{-1}
efficiently.

This allows us to efficiently compute
f

in place
. Applying this to the uniform superposition over
\{1,2,\dots,N-\absolutevalue{R}\}
produces the desired superposition.

2.

Next, compute the function that maps
(y,x,R)
to
R\cup\{(x,y)\}
(where
R
and
R\cup\{(x,y)\}
are represented as sorted lists of ordered pairs). This step corresponds to the map

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{R}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
y\not\in\imaginary(R)\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{x}\ket*{R}\mapsto\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{R}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
y\not\in\imaginary(R)\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{x}\ket*{R}\ket*{R \cup\{(x,y)\}}.

(A.4)

3.

Finally, use the
\ket*{y}\ket*{R \cup\{(x,y)\})}
register to uncompute the
\ket*{x}\ket*{R}
registers; note that
x,R
can be uniquely computed from
y,R\cup\{(x,y)\}
, since
y
is guaranteed to be outside
\imaginary(R)
. This corresponds to the map

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{R}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
y\not\in\imaginary(R)\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{x}\ket*{R}\ket*{R \cup\{(x,y)\}}\mapsto\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{R}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N],\\
y\not\in\imaginary(R)\end{subarray}}\ket*{y}\ket*{R \cup\{(x,y)\}},

(A.5)

which corresponds to the output of
V
.

A.3
Implementing forward and inverse queries

In
Section

8
, we defined a (symmetric) path-recording oracle
V
that simulates both forward and inverse queries to a Haar-random unitary. In this subsection, we describe how to implement this linear map efficiently. Recall that this linear map
V
is defined in terms of two helper linear maps
V^{L}
and
V^{R}
.

Definition 43

(left and right partial isometries)
.

[
Definition

25
, repeated]
Let
V^{L}
be the linear operator that acts as follows. For
x\in[N]
and
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2,\leq N-1}
,

\displaystyle V^{L}\cdot\ket*{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\coloneqq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\in[N]:\\
y\not\in\imaginary(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Im(L \cup R)}}}\ket*{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{L \cup\{(x,y)\}}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(A.6)

Define
V^{R}
to be the linear operator such that for all
y\in[N]
and
(L,R)\in\mathcal{R}^{2,\leq N-1}
,

\displaystyle V^{R}\cdot\ket*{y}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{R}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}\coloneqq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\in[N]:\\
x\not\in\Dom(L\cup R)\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N-\absolutevalue{\Dom(L \cup R)}}}\ket*{x}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{A}}}}\ket*{L}_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{L}}}}\ket*{R \cup\{(x, y)\} }_{{{\color[rgb]{0.25,0.25,0.25}\mathsf{R}}}}.

(A.7)

Efficient implementation of forward queries to
V^{L}
and
V^{R}
can be done similarly to
Section

A.2
. Now, let
U^{L}
denote the efficient unitary implicit in the procedure described in
Section

A.2
, which satisfies the guarantee that
U^{L}\ket*{x}\ket*{R}\ket*{0^m}=(V^{L}\ket*{x}\ket*{R})\otimes\ket*{0^{m'}}
, where
m
and
m^{\prime}
denotes the number of ancilla qubits in the input and output respectively. Define
U^{R}
similarly. Technically, the number of ancillas depends on the size of
L
and
R
. However, we can always assume that the sizes of
L
and
R
are upper bounded by the number of queries so far, and so we can also use this to bound the size of the ancillas needed to implement the
t
-th query.

Implementing
V^{L,\dagger},V^{R,\dagger}

Since we can efficiently implement
U^{L}
, we can also implement its inverse
U^{L,\dagger}
. Note that on states of the form
\ket*{\psi}\ket*{0^{m'}}
, where
\ket*{\psi}
is in the image of
V^{L}
, applying
U^{L,\dagger}
produces the output state
(V^{L,\dagger}\ket*{\psi})\ket*{0^m}
. Thus, we can use
U^{L,\dagger}
to implement
V^{L,\dagger}
.

Implementing coherent measurements on
V^{L}\cdot V^{L,\dagger}
and
V^{R}\cdot V^{R,\dagger}

Before we describe how we implement
V
